# Metric characterization across the dysts catalogue

Generated by `quarctest.characterize_series` over ensembles written by
`python/export_dysts.py`. Regenerate with:

```
python python/export_dysts.py --out tests/reports/dysts --R 100 --N 16000
matlab -batch "addpath('tests/matlab'); [S,p] = quarctest.characterize_series(R=100); quarctest.write_characterization(S,p,Tag='dysts')"
```

## What this is

For every system, 100 realizations of 16000 samples are read from the exported
ensemble: realization 1 starts at the published initial condition, the rest
are drawn on the attractor and relaxed, all sampled at 40 points per dominant
period. The full metric battery is run on each; the tables below report the
distribution of each metric across that ensemble.

**Nothing here passes or fails.** dysts (Gilpin 2021) publishes two of these
quantities per system, the largest Lyapunov exponent and the correlation
dimension, both computed from the linearized equations by integrating the
tangent space, and rows for those are marked `validated`. Every other row is
marked `characterized`: it is a measurement of this library's estimator on
this system, and no reference value exists to judge it against. Inventing a
tolerance for those would be a number with no provenance presented as a
criterion.

## Protocol

| parameter | value |
|---|---|
| realizations per system | 100 |
| samples per realization | 16000 |
| series source | `python/export_dysts.py`, dysts' own integrators |
| IC draw | on the attractor, relaxed; see `tests/reports/dysts/manifest.json` |
| realization 1 | the published initial condition, verbatim |
| sampling | 40 samples per dominant period, fixed by the export |
| embedding | delay from `ami`, dimension from `fnn`, per realization |
| spread statistic | MAD about the median, unscaled |

Every dysts system is a flow observed through one bounded component, so the
embedding is genuinely unknown and is estimated per realization, the way a
user would estimate it.

## Systems with an incomplete ensemble

A realization is dropped when its series is degenerate -- the orbit collapsed,
diverged, or produced an observable that is not bounded and recurrent. Where
the loss is large, **the surviving realizations are a sample conditioned on
not degenerating**, and the spread reported for that system describes a
narrower quantity than it does elsewhere. Read those rows accordingly.

| system | category | usable | of |
|---|---|---|---|
| `HyperLu` | dysts_flow | 97 | 100 |
| `Sakarya` | dysts_flow | 97 | 100 |
| `SprottJerk` | dysts_flow | 99 | 100 |
